Use when building real-time calls, huddles, presence-aware spaces, or any feature where multiple users share an audio/video channel. The platform provisions LiveKit-backed rooms, mints participant tokens server-side, and returns the LiveKit URL — clients use the LiveKit JS SDK with the issued token. Critical: never mint tokens in the browser; the token is a credential and must be server-issued per-participant. Returns `null` from `mediaUrl()` when media isn't configured for the project — handle that as a feature flag, not an error.
